## 👤 **Kirill**

Дай полный исправленный код
import { useCallback, useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  ReactFlow,
  ReactFlowProvider,
  Background,
  Controls,
  MiniMap,
  addEdge,
  useNodesState,
  useEdgesState,
  useReactFlow,
} from '@xyflow/react';
import type { Connection, Edge, Node } from '@xyflow/react';
import '@xyflow/react/dist/style.css';
import { MinimalNodePalette } from './components/MinimalNodePalette';
import { CustomNode } from './components/CustomNode';
import { PropertiesPanel } from './components/PropertiesPanel';
import { CanvasToolbar } from './components/CanvasToolbar';
import { generateProcess, saveProcess, getProcess, listProcesses } from './api/processApi';
import { useBpmnLayout } from '../../engines/BpmnLayoutEngine';
import { ProcessValidator } from '../../engines/ProcessValidator';
import { useAutoLayout } from './hooks/useAutoLayout';
import { useSimpleLayout } from './hooks/useSimpleLayout';
import { useSmartLayout } from './hooks/useSmartLayout';
import { useSemanticLayout } from './hooks/useSemanticLayout';
import { useCollisionLayout } from './hooks/useCollisionLayout';
import { useProcessExecution } from './hooks/useProcessExecution';
import './process-designer.css';

const nodeTypes = {
  custom: CustomNode,
};

function ProcessDesignerContent() {
  const [nodes, setNodes, onNodesChange] = useNodesState([]);
  const [edges, setEdges, onEdgesChange] = useEdgesState([]);
  const [selectedNode, setSelectedNode] = useState<Node | null>(null);
  const [requirement, setRequirement] = useState('');
  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [currentProcessId, setCurrentProcessId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [processList, setProcessList] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedProcessId, setSelectedProcessId] = useState<string>('');
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<{ name: string; path: string }[]>([]);
  const [selectedProject, setSelectedProject] = useState('');
  const [layoutDirection, setLayoutDirection] = useState<'DOWN' | 'UP' | 'LEFT' | 'RIGHT'>('DOWN');
  const [layoutQuality, setLayoutQuality] = useState<'fast' | 'balanced' | 'quality'>('balanced');
  const [edgeRouting, setEdgeRouting] = useState<'ORTHOGONAL' | 'POLYLINE' | 'SPLINES'>('ORTHOGONAL');
  const [layoutEngine, setLayoutEngine] = useState<'elk' | 'simple' | 'semantic' | 'bpmn'>('bpmn');
  
  // Terminal state
  const [terminalLogs, setTerminalLogs] = useState<string[]>(['> Ready. Select a process and click Run.']);
  const [promptInput, setPromptInput] = useState('');
  const terminalEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const { execution, startExecution, approveNode } = useProcessExecution(currentProcessId);

  const { screenToFlowPosition } = useReactFlow();

  // Selection state
  const [selectedNodesIds, setSelectedNodesIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedEdgesIds, setSelectedEdgesIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

  // Clipboard state
  const [clipboard, setClipboard] = useState<{ nodes: Node[]; edges: Edge[] } | null>(null);

  const { bpmnLayout } = useBpmnLayout();
  const { autoLayout } = useAutoLayout();
  const { simpleLayout: simpleLayoutFn } = useSimpleLayout();
  const { smartLayout } = useSmartLayout();
  const { semanticLayout } = useSemanticLayout();
  const { resolveCollisions } = useCollisionLayout(); 

  const addTerminalLog = (message: string) => {
    setTerminalLogs(prev => [...prev, message]);
  };

  // Handle selection change
  const onSelectionChange = useCallback((params: { nodes: Node[]; edges: Edge[] }) => {
    setSelectedNodesIds(params.nodes.map(n => n.id));
    setSelectedEdgesIds(params.edges.map(e => e.id));
  }, []);

  // Copy function
  const copyToClipboard = useCallback((selectedNodes: Node[], selectedEdges: Edge[]) => {
    const copiedNodes = selectedNodes.map(node => ({
      ...node,
      id: `copy-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
      position: { x: node.position.x + 50, y: node.position.y + 50 },
      selected: false,
    }));
    setClipboard({ nodes: copiedNodes, edges: selectedEdges });
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Copied ${selectedNodes.length} nodes`);
  }, [addTerminalLog]);

  // Paste function
  const pasteFromClipboard = useCallback(() => {
    if (!clipboard) return null;
    return {
      nodes: clipboard.nodes.map(node => ({
        ...node,
        id: `paste-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
        position: { x: node.position.x + 20, y: node.position.y + 20 },
        selected: true,
      })),
      edges: clipboard.edges.map(edge => ({
        ...edge,
        id: `${edge.source}-${edge.target}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
        selected: false,
      })),
    };
  }, [clipboard]);

  // Update edges style based on routing type
  const updateEdgesStyle = useCallback((routing: 'ORTHOGONAL' | 'POLYLINE' | 'SPLINES') => {
    setEdges(prevEdges => 
      prevEdges.map(edge => ({
        ...edge,
        animated: true,
        style: { stroke: '#5A6E8A', strokeWidth: 2 },
        ...(routing === 'SPLINES' && { type: 'default' }),    // плавные
        ...(routing === 'POLYLINE' && { type: 'step' }),         // ортогональные
        ...(routing === 'ORTHOGONAL' && { type: 'straight' }),   // прямые линии
      }))
    );
  }, [setEdges]);

  // Handle keyboard shortcuts
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Ctrl+C / Cmd+C
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'c') {
        const selectedNodesList = nodes.filter(n => selectedNodesIds.includes(n.id));
        const selectedEdgesList = edges.filter(e => selectedEdgesIds.includes(e.id));
        if (selectedNodesList.length > 0) {
          e.preventDefault();
          copyToClipboard(selectedNodesList, selectedEdgesList);
        }
      }
      
      // Ctrl+V / Cmd+V
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'v') {
        const pasted = pasteFromClipboard();
        if (pasted && pasted.nodes.length > 0) {
          e.preventDefault();
          setNodes(prev => [...prev, ...pasted.nodes]);
          setEdges(prev => [...prev, ...pasted.edges]);
          addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Pasted ${pasted.nodes.length} nodes`);
        }
      }
      
      // Delete / Backspace
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        if (selectedNodesIds.length > 0 || selectedEdgesIds.length > 0) {
          e.preventDefault();
          setNodes(prev => prev.filter(n => !selectedNodesIds.includes(n.id)));
          setEdges(prev => prev.filter(e => !selectedEdgesIds.includes(e.id)));
          addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Deleted ${selectedNodesIds.length} nodes and ${selectedEdgesIds.length} edges`);
          setSelectedNodesIds([]);
          setSelectedEdgesIds([]);
        }
      }
    };
    
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [nodes, edges, selectedNodesIds, selectedEdgesIds, copyToClipboard, pasteFromClipboard, setNodes, setEdges, addTerminalLog]);

  // Update edge style when routing changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (edges.length > 0) {
      updateEdgesStyle(edgeRouting);
    }
  }, [edgeRouting, updateEdgesStyle, edges.length]);

  const handleSendPrompt = () => {
    if (!promptInput.trim()) return;
    addTerminalLog(`> ${promptInput}`);
    setPromptInput('');
    const inputDiv = document.querySelector('.terminal-prompt-input');
    if (inputDiv) inputDiv.innerHTML = '';
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    terminalEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [terminalLogs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (execution?.status === 'running') {
      addTerminalLog('[INFO] Execution started...');
    }
    if (execution?.nodeStatuses) {
      Object.entries(execution.nodeStatuses).forEach(([nodeId, status]) => {
        if (status === 'completed') {
          addTerminalLog(`[OK] ${nodeId} completed`);
        } else if (status === 'failed') {
          addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] ${nodeId} failed`);
        } else if (status === 'running') {
          addTerminalLog(`[RUN] ${nodeId} running...`);
        }
      });
    }
  }, [execution]);

  const loadProjects = async () => {
    const mockProjects = [
      { name: 'Kymatics Core', path: '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub' },
      { name: 'Tactical Command', path: '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3' },
      { name: 'AI Farm', path: '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm' },
    ];
    setProjects(mockProjects);
  };

  const handleSelectProject = () => {
    if (!selectedProject) {
      addTerminalLog('[FAIL] Please select a project');
      return;
    }
    const project = projects.find(p => p.path === selectedProject);
    addTerminalLog(`[OK] Project selected: ${project?.name} at ${project?.path}`);
  };

  const handleExport = () => {
    const processData = {
      name: currentProcessId || "untitled",
      nodes: nodes.map(node => ({
        id: node.id,
        type: node.type,
        position: node.position,
        data: node.data
      })),
      edges: edges.map(edge => ({
        id: edge.id,
        source: edge.source,
        target: edge.target,
        sourceHandle: edge.sourceHandle,
        targetHandle: edge.targetHandle
      }))
    };
    const dataStr = JSON.stringify(processData, null, 2);
    const blob = new Blob([dataStr], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `process_${Date.now()}.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    addTerminalLog('[OK] Process exported to JSON');
  };

  const handleImport = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      try {
        const processData = JSON.parse(e.target?.result as string);
        setNodes(processData.nodes);
        setEdges(processData.edges);
        addTerminalLog(`[OK] Imported process from ${file.name}`);
        alert('Process imported successfully!');
      } catch (error) {
        addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Failed to import: ${error}`);
        alert('Failed to import process: ' + error);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (execution?.nodeStatuses) {
      setNodes(prevNodes => 
        prevNodes.map(node => ({
          ...node,
          data: {
            ...node.data,
            status: execution.nodeStatuses[node.id] || 'pending'
          }
        }))
      );
    }
  }, [execution?.nodeStatuses, setNodes]);

  const onConnect = useCallback(
    (params: Connection) => {
      console.log('Connection params:', params);
      
      let edgeType = 'straight';
      if (edgeRouting === 'POLYLINE') edgeType = 'step';
      if (edgeRouting === 'SPLINES') edgeType = 'default';
      
      // Сохраняем sourceHandle и targetHandle как есть (они будут с правильными ID)
      const newEdge = {
        ...params,
        id: `${params.source}-${params.target}-${Date.now()}`,
        sourceHandle: params.sourceHandle || 'source-right',
        targetHandle: params.targetHandle || 'target-left',
        type: edgeType,
        animated: true,
        style: { stroke: '#5A6E8A', strokeWidth: 2 }
      };
      
      console.log('Created edge:', newEdge);
      setEdges((eds) => addEdge(newEdge, eds));
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Connected ${params.source} -> ${params.target}`);
    },
    [setEdges, edgeRouting]
  );

  const onDragOver = useCallback((event: React.DragEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
  }, []);

  const onDrop = useCallback(
    (event: React.DragEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const nodeDataStr = event.dataTransfer.getData('application/reactflow');
      if (!nodeDataStr) return;
      const nodeData = JSON.parse(nodeDataStr);
      
      const position = screenToFlowPosition({
        x: event.clientX,
        y: event.clientY,
      });
      
      const newNode: Node = {
        id: `${Date.now()}`,
        type: 'custom',
        position,
        data: { 
          label: nodeData.label,
          node_type: nodeData.node_type,
          status: 'pending',
          bg_color: nodeData.bg_color,
          icon: nodeData.icon
        },
      };
      setNodes((nds) => nds.concat(newNode));
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Added node: ${nodeData.label}`);
    },
    [screenToFlowPosition, setNodes]
  );

  const handleGenerate = async () => {
    if (!requirement.trim()) {
      addTerminalLog('[FAIL] Please enter a requirement');
      alert('Please enter a requirement');
      return;
    }
    setIsGenerating(true);
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Generating process from: "${requirement}"`);
    try {
      const result = await generateProcess(requirement);
      const nodesWithStatus = result.nodes.map((node: any) => ({
        ...node,
        type: 'custom',
        data: { ...node.data, status: 'pending' }
      }));
      setNodes(nodesWithStatus);
      setEdges(result.edges);
      const saveResult = await saveProcess(
        `AI Generated: ${requirement.slice(0, 50)}`,
        requirement,
        nodesWithStatus,
        result.edges,
        result.yaml,
        result.mermaid
      );
      setCurrentProcessId(saveResult.id);
      await loadProcessList();
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Process generated and saved with ID: ${saveResult.id}`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to generate process:', error);
      addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Failed to generate process: ${error}`);
      alert('Failed to generate process');
    } finally {
      setIsGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    addTerminalLog('[INFO] Saving process...');
    try {
      if (!currentProcessId) {
        const result = await saveProcess('Untitled Process', '', nodes, edges, '', '');
        setCurrentProcessId(result.id);
      } else {
        await saveProcess(
          processList.find(p => p.id === currentProcessId)?.name || 'Untitled',
          '',
          nodes,
          edges,
          '',
          ''
        );
      }
      await loadProcessList();
      addTerminalLog('[OK] Process saved successfully');
      alert('Process saved!');
    } catch (error) {
      addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Failed to save: ${error}`);
      alert('Failed to save');
    }
  };

  const loadProcessList = async () => {
    try {
      const result = await listProcesses();
      setProcessList(result.processes || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load processes:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadProcess = async (processId: string) => {
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Loading process: ${processId}`);
    try {
      const process = await getProcess(processId);
      const nodesWithType = process.nodes.map((n: any) => ({ 
        ...n, 
        type: 'custom',
        data: { ...n.data, status: 'pending' } 
      }));
      setNodes(nodesWithType);
      setEdges(process.edges);
      setCurrentProcessId(process.id);
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Loaded process: ${process.name}`);
      
      // Apply current edge routing style to loaded edges
      setTimeout(() => updateEdgesStyle(edgeRouting), 100);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load process:', error);
      addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Failed to load: ${error}`);
    }
  };

  const handleExecute = async () => {
    if (!currentProcessId) {
      addTerminalLog('[FAIL] Please save the process first');
      alert('Save the process first');
      return;
    }
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Executing process: ${currentProcessId}`);
    await startExecution();
  };

  const handleUpdateNode = (nodeId: string, newData: any) => {
    setNodes((nds) =>
      nds.map((node) =>
        node.id === nodeId ? { ...node, data: newData } : node
      )
    );
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Updated node: ${newData.label || nodeId}`);
  };

  const handleApprove = async (nodeId: string) => {
    if (!execution) return;
    await approveNode({
      nodeId,
      nodeLabel: nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId)?.data.label || '',
      taskId: execution.executionId || '',
      executionId: execution.executionId || ''
    });
    addTerminalLog(`[OK] Approved node: ${nodeId}`);
  };

  const handleAutoLayout = async () => {
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Running ${layoutEngine} layout (${layoutDirection})...`);
    
    if (nodes.length === 0) {
      addTerminalLog('[WARN] No nodes to layout');
      return;
    }
    
    console.log('=== LAYOUT DEBUG ===');
    console.log('Engine:', layoutEngine);
    console.log('Nodes before layout:', nodes.map(n => ({ 
      id: n.id, 
      type: n.data.node_type,
      x: n.position.x, 
      y: n.position.y 
    })));
    console.log('Edges:', edges.map(e => ({ source: e.source, target: e.target })));
    
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
    
    let layoutedNodes: Node[];
    
    if (layoutEngine === 'semantic') {
      const resetNodes = nodes.map(node => ({
        ...node,
        position: { x: 0, y: 0 }
      }));
      layoutedNodes = await semanticLayout(resetNodes, edges, {
        horizontalSpacing: 320,
        verticalSpacing: 180,
        branchSpacing: 250
      });
    } else if (layoutEngine === 'elk') {
      layoutedNodes = await smartLayout(nodes, edges, {
        direction: layoutDirection,
        quality: layoutQuality,
        edgeRouting: edgeRouting,
        nodeNodeSpacing: 100,
        layerSpacing: 150,
      });
    // В handleAutoLayout для sbpmnLayout:
    } else if (layoutEngine === 'bpmn') {
      layoutedNodes = await bpmnLayout(nodes, edges, {
        horizontalSpacing: 250,
        verticalSpacing: 100,
        branchSpacing: 80,
        parallelSpacing: 60,
      });
    }
        
    console.log('Nodes after layout:', layoutedNodes.map(n => ({ 
      id: n.id, 
      type: n.data.node_type,
      x: n.position.x, 
      y: n.position.y 
    })));
    
    const finalNodes = resolveCollisions(layoutedNodes, edges, 20);
    
    setNodes(finalNodes);
    setTimeout(() => updateEdgesStyle(edgeRouting), 100);
    
    addTerminalLog('[OK] Layout completed with collision resolution');
  };

  const handleClearTerminal = () => {
    setTerminalLogs(['> Terminal cleared']);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadProjects();
    loadProcessList();
  }, []);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedProcessId) {
      loadProcess(selectedProcessId);
    }
  }, [selectedProcessId]);
  
  // Force update node dimensions before layout when quality changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (nodes.length > 0 && layoutQuality !== 'fast') {
      // Trigger a re-render to update measured dimensions
      setNodes(prev => [...prev]);
    }
  }, [layoutQuality]);

  // Initialize resize for all panels
  useEffect(() => {
    const leftPanel = document.querySelector('.node-palette-minimal');
    const rightPanel = document.querySelector('.right-panel');
    const terminalPanel = document.querySelector('.terminal-panel');
    
    let startX, startWidth;
    let startY, startHeight;
    let activePanel = null;
    
    const onMouseMove = (e) => {
      if (!activePanel) return;
      if (activePanel === leftPanel) {
        const newWidth = startWidth + (e.clientX - startX);
        if (newWidth >= 180 && newWidth <= 400) {
          activePanel.style.width = newWidth + 'px';
        }
      } else if (activePanel === rightPanel) {
        const newWidth = startWidth + (startX - e.clientX);
        if (newWidth >= 200 && newWidth <= 500) {
          activePanel.style.width = newWidth + 'px';
        }
      } else if (activePanel === terminalPanel) {
        const newHeight = startHeight + (startY - e.clientY);
        if (newHeight >= 120 && newHeight <= 600) {
          activePanel.style.height = newHeight + 'px';
        }
      }
    };
    
    const onMouseUp = () => {
      activePanel = null;
      document.body.style.cursor = '';
      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
    };
    
    const createResizeHandle = (panel, direction, cursor) => {
      const handle = document.createElement('div');
      handle.className = `resize-handle resize-handle-${direction}`;
      handle.style.cssText = `position: absolute; 
        ${direction === 'right' ? 'right: -4px; top: 0; bottom: 0; width: 6px;' : 
          direction === 'left' ? 'left: -4px; top: 0; bottom: 0; width: 6px;' :
          direction === 'top' ? 'top: -4px; left: 0; right: 0; height: 6px;' : 
          'bottom: -4px; left: 0; right: 0; height: 6px;'} 
        cursor: ${cursor}; z-index: 100;`;
      handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        activePanel = panel;
        startX = e.clientX;
        startY = e.clientY;
        startWidth = panel.offsetWidth;
        startHeight = panel.offsetHeight;
        document.body.style.cursor = cursor;
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
        document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
      });
      panel.style.position = 'relative';
      panel.appendChild(handle);
    };
    
    if (leftPanel && !leftPanel.querySelector('.resize-handle')) createResizeHandle(leftPanel, 'right', 'ew-resize');
    if (rightPanel && !rightPanel.querySelector('.resize-handle')) createResizeHandle(rightPanel, 'left', 'ew-resize');
    if (terminalPanel && !terminalPanel.querySelector('.resize-handle')) createResizeHandle(terminalPanel, 'top', 'ns-resize');
    
    return () => {
      const handles = document.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle');
      handles.forEach(h => h.removeEventListener('mousedown', () => {}));
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="process-designer">
      <div className="process-designer-toolbar">
        <h1 className="process-designer-title">Process Designer</h1>
        <div className="project-selector">
          <select className="project-select" value={selectedProject} onChange={(e) => setSelectedProject(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">Select project...</option>
            {projects.map((project) => (
              <option key={project.path} value={project.path}>{project.name}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <button className="project-select-btn" onClick={handleSelectProject}>Open</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="process-designer-main">
        <MinimalNodePalette />
        
        <div className="canvas-terminal-container">
          <CanvasToolbar
            onLayout={handleAutoLayout}
            layoutDirection={layoutDirection}
            onLayoutDirectionChange={setLayoutDirection}
            layoutQuality={layoutQuality}
            onLayoutQualityChange={setLayoutQuality}
            edgeRouting={edgeRouting}
            onEdgeRoutingChange={setEdgeRouting}
            layoutEngine={layoutEngine}
            onLayoutEngineChange={setLayoutEngine}
            onClear={handleClearTerminal}
            onStop={() => addTerminalLog('Stop pressed')}
            onSave={handleSave}
            onRun={handleExecute}
            nodes={nodes}      // <-- добавить
            edges={edges}      // <-- добавить
          />
          
          <div className="process-designer-canvas" onDragOver={onDragOver} onDrop={onDrop}>
            <ReactFlow
              nodes={nodes}
              edges={edges}
              onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
              onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
              onConnect={onConnect}
              onNodeClick={(_, node) => setSelectedNode(node)}
              nodeTypes={nodeTypes}            
              defaultEdgeOptions={{
                animated: true,
                style: { stroke: '#5A6E8A', strokeWidth: 2 }
              }}
              nodeDragThreshold={0}
              nodesDraggable={true}
              nodesConnectable={true}
              snapToGrid={true}
              snapGrid={[15, 15]}
              selectionOnDrag={true}
              panOnDrag={[1, 2]}
              deleteKeyCode={null}
              multiSelectionKeyCode="Shift"
              selectionMode="partial"
              onSelectionChange={onSelectionChange}
            >
              <Background color="#1A2538" gap={20} size={1} />
              <Controls className="process-designer-controls" />
              <MiniMap className="process-designer-minimap" nodeColor="#4A9FFF" maskColor="rgba(10, 15, 26, 0.8)" />
            </ReactFlow>
          </div>

          <div className="terminal-panel">
            <div className="terminal-toolbar">
              <div className="terminal-actions-right">
                <button className="terminal-btn" onClick={handleExport}>Export JSON</button>
                <label className="terminal-btn" style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                  Import JSON
                  <input type="file" accept=".json" onChange={handleImport} style={{ display: 'none' }} />
                </label>
                <button className="terminal-btn" onClick={() => addTerminalLog('[INFO] Share feature coming soon')}>Share</button>
                <select className="terminal-process-select" value={selectedProcessId} onChange={(e) => setSelectedProcessId(e.target.value)}>
                  <option value="">Select process...</option>
                  {processList.map((p: any) => (
                    <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="terminal-content">
              {terminalLogs.map((log, idx) => (
                <div key={idx} className="terminal-log-line">{log}</div>
              ))}
              <div ref={terminalEndRef} />
            </div>
            
            <div className="terminal-prompt-container">
              <div
                className="terminal-prompt-input"
                contentEditable
                suppressContentEditableWarning
                data-placeholder="Describe your process in natural language..."
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const text = e.currentTarget.innerText.trim();
                    if (text) {
                      addTerminalLog(`> ${text}`);
                      setPromptInput(text);
                      e.currentTarget.innerText = '';
                    }
                  }
                }}
                onInput={(e) => setPromptInput(e.currentTarget.innerText)}
              />
              
              <div className="terminal-prompt-actions">
                <button className="terminal-attach-btn" title="Attach">⸬</button>
                <button className="terminal-send-btn" onClick={handleSendPrompt} title="Send">
                  <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" strokeWidth="2">
                    <path d="M12 5v14M5 12l7-7 7 7"/>
                  </svg>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>  
        </div>

        <div className="right-panel">
          <div className="right-panel-tabs">
            <button className="right-panel-tab active" data-tab="agents">Agents</button>
            <button className="right-panel-tab" data-tab="info">Info</button>
            <button className="right-panel-tab" data-tab="settings">Settings</button>
          </div>
          <div className="right-panel-content">
            <div className="agents-tree" id="agentsTab">
              <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">▼</span><span className="tree-label">core_agents</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
              <div className="tree-children">
                <div className="tree-item selected"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">architect_agent</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
                <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">coder_agent</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
                <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">validator_agent</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
              </div>
              <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">▶</span><span className="tree-label">project_agents</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
              <div className="tree-children">
                <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">frontend_developer</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
                <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">api_designer</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div id="infoTab" style={{ display: 'none' }}>
              {selectedNode ? (
                <>
                  <div className="info-field"><div className="info-label">Name</div><div className="info-value">{selectedNode.data.label || 'Unnamed'}</div></div>
                  <div className="info-field"><div className="info-label">Type</div><div className="info-value">{selectedNode.data.node_type}</div></div>
                  <div className="info-field"><div className="info-label">Status</div><div className="info-value">{selectedNode.data.status || 'pending'}</div></div>
                  <div className="info-field"><div className="info-label">ID</div><div className="info-value">{selectedNode.id}</div></div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="info-value" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Select a node to see info</div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div id="settingsTab" style={{ display: 'none' }}>
              <div className="setting-item"><label className="setting-label">Default Model</label><input type="text" className="setting-input" defaultValue="qwen2.5-coder:7b" /></div>
              <div className="setting-item"><label className="setting-label">Temperature</label><input type="range" min="0" max="2" step="0.1" defaultValue="0.7" style={{ width: '100%' }} /></div>
              <div className="setting-item"><label className="setting-label">Max Tokens</label><input type="number" className="setting-input" defaultValue="2000" /></div>
              <div className="setting-item"><label className="setting-label">Ollama URL</label><input type="text" className="setting-input" defaultValue="http://localhost:11434" /></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {execution && execution.status === 'running' && (
          <div className="process-designer-execution-panel">
            <h3>Execution Running</h3>
            <p>Current node: {execution.currentNodeId}</p>
            <div className="execution-progress">
              {Object.entries(execution.nodeStatuses).map(([nodeId, status]) => (
                <div key={nodeId} className={`execution-node-status status-${status}`}>{nodeId}: {status}</div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {selectedNode && selectedNode.data.node_type === 'approval' && (
          <div className="process-designer-inspector">
            <h3 className="process-designer-inspector-title">Approval Required</h3>
            <button onClick={() => handleApprove(selectedNode.id)} className="process-designer-approve-btn">Approve Node</button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ProcessDesignerPage() {
  return (
    <ReactFlowProvider>
      <ProcessDesignerContent />
    </ReactFlowProvider>
  );
}

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Вот полный исправленный код с добавленным валидатором:

```typescript
import { useCallback, useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  ReactFlow,
  ReactFlowProvider,
  Background,
  Controls,
  MiniMap,
  addEdge,
  useNodesState,
  useEdgesState,
  useReactFlow,
} from '@xyflow/react';
import type { Connection, Edge, Node } from '@xyflow/react';
import '@xyflow/react/dist/style.css';
import { MinimalNodePalette } from './components/MinimalNodePalette';
import { CustomNode } from './components/CustomNode';
import { PropertiesPanel } from './components/PropertiesPanel';
import { CanvasToolbar } from './components/CanvasToolbar';
import { generateProcess, saveProcess, getProcess, listProcesses } from './api/processApi';
import { useBpmnLayout } from '../../engines/BpmnLayoutEngine';
import { ProcessValidator } from '../../engines/ProcessValidator';
import { useAutoLayout } from './hooks/useAutoLayout';
import { useSimpleLayout } from './hooks/useSimpleLayout';
import { useSmartLayout } from './hooks/useSmartLayout';
import { useSemanticLayout } from './hooks/useSemanticLayout';
import { useCollisionLayout } from './hooks/useCollisionLayout';
import { useProcessExecution } from './hooks/useProcessExecution';
import './process-designer.css';

const nodeTypes = {
  custom: CustomNode,
};

function ProcessDesignerContent() {
  const [nodes, setNodes, onNodesChange] = useNodesState([]);
  const [edges, setEdges, onEdgesChange] = useEdgesState([]);
  const [selectedNode, setSelectedNode] = useState<Node | null>(null);
  const [requirement, setRequirement] = useState('');
  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [currentProcessId, setCurrentProcessId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [processList, setProcessList] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [selectedProcessId, setSelectedProcessId] = useState<string>('');
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<{ name: string; path: string }[]>([]);
  const [selectedProject, setSelectedProject] = useState('');
  const [layoutDirection, setLayoutDirection] = useState<'DOWN' | 'UP' | 'LEFT' | 'RIGHT'>('DOWN');
  const [layoutQuality, setLayoutQuality] = useState<'fast' | 'balanced' | 'quality'>('balanced');
  const [edgeRouting, setEdgeRouting] = useState<'ORTHOGONAL' | 'POLYLINE' | 'SPLINES'>('ORTHOGONAL');
  const [layoutEngine, setLayoutEngine] = useState<'elk' | 'simple' | 'semantic' | 'bpmn'>('bpmn');
  
  // Terminal state
  const [terminalLogs, setTerminalLogs] = useState<string[]>(['> Ready. Select a process and click Run.']);
  const [promptInput, setPromptInput] = useState('');
  const terminalEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const { execution, startExecution, approveNode } = useProcessExecution(currentProcessId);

  const { screenToFlowPosition } = useReactFlow();

  // Selection state
  const [selectedNodesIds, setSelectedNodesIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedEdgesIds, setSelectedEdgesIds] = useState<string[]>([]);

  // Clipboard state
  const [clipboard, setClipboard] = useState<{ nodes: Node[]; edges: Edge[] } | null>(null);

  // Валидатор
  const validator = useMemo(() => new ProcessValidator(), []);

  const { bpmnLayout } = useBpmnLayout();
  const { autoLayout } = useAutoLayout();
  const { simpleLayout: simpleLayoutFn } = useSimpleLayout();
  const { smartLayout } = useSmartLayout();
  const { semanticLayout } = useSemanticLayout();
  const { resolveCollisions } = useCollisionLayout(); 

  const addTerminalLog = (message: string) => {
    setTerminalLogs(prev => [...prev, message]);
  };

  // Handle selection change
  const onSelectionChange = useCallback((params: { nodes: Node[]; edges: Edge[] }) => {
    setSelectedNodesIds(params.nodes.map(n => n.id));
    setSelectedEdgesIds(params.edges.map(e => e.id));
  }, []);

  // Copy function
  const copyToClipboard = useCallback((selectedNodes: Node[], selectedEdges: Edge[]) => {
    const copiedNodes = selectedNodes.map(node => ({
      ...node,
      id: `copy-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
      position: { x: node.position.x + 50, y: node.position.y + 50 },
      selected: false,
    }));
    setClipboard({ nodes: copiedNodes, edges: selectedEdges });
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Copied ${selectedNodes.length} nodes`);
  }, [addTerminalLog]);

  // Paste function
  const pasteFromClipboard = useCallback(() => {
    if (!clipboard) return null;
    return {
      nodes: clipboard.nodes.map(node => ({
        ...node,
        id: `paste-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
        position: { x: node.position.x + 20, y: node.position.y + 20 },
        selected: true,
      })),
      edges: clipboard.edges.map(edge => ({
        ...edge,
        id: `${edge.source}-${edge.target}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
        selected: false,
      })),
    };
  }, [clipboard]);

  // Update edges style based on routing type
  const updateEdgesStyle = useCallback((routing: 'ORTHOGONAL' | 'POLYLINE' | 'SPLINES') => {
    setEdges(prevEdges => 
      prevEdges.map(edge => ({
        ...edge,
        animated: true,
        style: { stroke: '#5A6E8A', strokeWidth: 2 },
        ...(routing === 'SPLINES' && { type: 'default' }),
        ...(routing === 'POLYLINE' && { type: 'step' }),
        ...(routing === 'ORTHOGONAL' && { type: 'straight' }),
      }))
    );
  }, [setEdges]);

  // Handle keyboard shortcuts
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Ctrl+C / Cmd+C
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'c') {
        const selectedNodesList = nodes.filter(n => selectedNodesIds.includes(n.id));
        const selectedEdgesList = edges.filter(e => selectedEdgesIds.includes(e.id));
        if (selectedNodesList.length > 0) {
          e.preventDefault();
          copyToClipboard(selectedNodesList, selectedEdgesList);
        }
      }
      
      // Ctrl+V / Cmd+V
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'v') {
        const pasted = pasteFromClipboard();
        if (pasted && pasted.nodes.length > 0) {
          e.preventDefault();
          setNodes(prev => [...prev, ...pasted.nodes]);
          setEdges(prev => [...prev, ...pasted.edges]);
          addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Pasted ${pasted.nodes.length} nodes`);
        }
      }
      
      // Delete / Backspace
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        if (selectedNodesIds.length > 0 || selectedEdgesIds.length > 0) {
          e.preventDefault();
          setNodes(prev => prev.filter(n => !selectedNodesIds.includes(n.id)));
          setEdges(prev => prev.filter(e => !selectedEdgesIds.includes(e.id)));
          addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Deleted ${selectedNodesIds.length} nodes and ${selectedEdgesIds.length} edges`);
          setSelectedNodesIds([]);
          setSelectedEdgesIds([]);
        }
      }
    };
    
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [nodes, edges, selectedNodesIds, selectedEdgesIds, copyToClipboard, pasteFromClipboard, setNodes, setEdges, addTerminalLog]);

  // Update edge style when routing changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (edges.length > 0) {
      updateEdgesStyle(edgeRouting);
    }
  }, [edgeRouting, updateEdgesStyle, edges.length]);

  const handleSendPrompt = () => {
    if (!promptInput.trim()) return;
    addTerminalLog(`> ${promptInput}`);
    setPromptInput('');
    const inputDiv = document.querySelector('.terminal-prompt-input');
    if (inputDiv) inputDiv.innerHTML = '';
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    terminalEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [terminalLogs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (execution?.status === 'running') {
      addTerminalLog('[INFO] Execution started...');
    }
    if (execution?.nodeStatuses) {
      Object.entries(execution.nodeStatuses).forEach(([nodeId, status]) => {
        if (status === 'completed') {
          addTerminalLog(`[OK] ${nodeId} completed`);
        } else if (status === 'failed') {
          addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] ${nodeId} failed`);
        } else if (status === 'running') {
          addTerminalLog(`[RUN] ${nodeId} running...`);
        }
      });
    }
  }, [execution]);

  const loadProjects = async () => {
    const mockProjects = [
      { name: 'Kymatics Core', path: '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub' },
      { name: 'Tactical Command', path: '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3' },
      { name: 'AI Farm', path: '/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm' },
    ];
    setProjects(mockProjects);
  };

  const handleSelectProject = () => {
    if (!selectedProject) {
      addTerminalLog('[FAIL] Please select a project');
      return;
    }
    const project = projects.find(p => p.path === selectedProject);
    addTerminalLog(`[OK] Project selected: ${project?.name} at ${project?.path}`);
  };

  const handleExport = () => {
    const processData = {
      name: currentProcessId || "untitled",
      nodes: nodes.map(node => ({
        id: node.id,
        type: node.type,
        position: node.position,
        data: node.data
      })),
      edges: edges.map(edge => ({
        id: edge.id,
        source: edge.source,
        target: edge.target,
        sourceHandle: edge.sourceHandle,
        targetHandle: edge.targetHandle
      }))
    };
    const dataStr = JSON.stringify(processData, null, 2);
    const blob = new Blob([dataStr], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `process_${Date.now()}.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    addTerminalLog('[OK] Process exported to JSON');
  };

  const handleImport = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      try {
        const processData = JSON.parse(e.target?.result as string);
        setNodes(processData.nodes);
        setEdges(processData.edges);
        addTerminalLog(`[OK] Imported process from ${file.name}`);
        alert('Process imported successfully!');
      } catch (error) {
        addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Failed to import: ${error}`);
        alert('Failed to import process: ' + error);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (execution?.nodeStatuses) {
      setNodes(prevNodes => 
        prevNodes.map(node => ({
          ...node,
          data: {
            ...node.data,
            status: execution.nodeStatuses[node.id] || 'pending'
          }
        }))
      );
    }
  }, [execution?.nodeStatuses, setNodes]);

  const onConnect = useCallback(
    (params: Connection) => {
      console.log('Connection params:', params);
      
      let edgeType = 'straight';
      if (edgeRouting === 'POLYLINE') edgeType = 'step';
      if (edgeRouting === 'SPLINES') edgeType = 'default';
      
      const newEdge = {
        ...params,
        id: `${params.source}-${params.target}-${Date.now()}`,
        sourceHandle: params.sourceHandle || 'source-right',
        targetHandle: params.targetHandle || 'target-left',
        type: edgeType,
        animated: true,
        style: { stroke: '#5A6E8A', strokeWidth: 2 }
      };
      
      console.log('Created edge:', newEdge);
      setEdges((eds) => addEdge(newEdge, eds));
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Connected ${params.source} -> ${params.target}`);
    },
    [setEdges, edgeRouting]
  );

  const onDragOver = useCallback((event: React.DragEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';
  }, []);

  const onDrop = useCallback(
    (event: React.DragEvent) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      const nodeDataStr = event.dataTransfer.getData('application/reactflow');
      if (!nodeDataStr) return;
      const nodeData = JSON.parse(nodeDataStr);
      
      const position = screenToFlowPosition({
        x: event.clientX,
        y: event.clientY,
      });
      
      const newNode: Node = {
        id: `${Date.now()}`,
        type: 'custom',
        position,
        data: { 
          label: nodeData.label,
          node_type: nodeData.node_type,
          status: 'pending',
          bg_color: nodeData.bg_color,
          icon: nodeData.icon
        },
      };
      setNodes((nds) => nds.concat(newNode));
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Added node: ${nodeData.label}`);
    },
    [screenToFlowPosition, setNodes]
  );

  const handleGenerate = async () => {
    if (!requirement.trim()) {
      addTerminalLog('[FAIL] Please enter a requirement');
      alert('Please enter a requirement');
      return;
    }
    setIsGenerating(true);
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Generating process from: "${requirement}"`);
    try {
      const result = await generateProcess(requirement);
      const nodesWithStatus = result.nodes.map((node: any) => ({
        ...node,
        type: 'custom',
        data: { ...node.data, status: 'pending' }
      }));
      
      // Валидация сгенерированного процесса
      const validation = validator.validate(nodesWithStatus, result.edges);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        addTerminalLog('[WARN] Generated process has issues:');
        validation.errors.forEach(err => addTerminalLog(`  - ${err}`));
      } else {
        addTerminalLog('[OK] Generated process structure is valid');
      }
      
      setNodes(nodesWithStatus);
      setEdges(result.edges);
      const saveResult = await saveProcess(
        `AI Generated: ${requirement.slice(0, 50)}`,
        requirement,
        nodesWithStatus,
        result.edges,
        result.yaml,
        result.mermaid
      );
      setCurrentProcessId(saveResult.id);
      await loadProcessList();
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Process generated and saved with ID: ${saveResult.id}`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to generate process:', error);
      addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Failed to generate process: ${error}`);
      alert('Failed to generate process');
    } finally {
      setIsGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    addTerminalLog('[INFO] Saving process...');
    
    // Валидация перед сохранением
    const validation = validator.validate(nodes, edges);
    if (!validation.valid) {
      addTerminalLog('[FAIL] Cannot save: Invalid process structure');
      validation.errors.forEach(err => addTerminalLog(`  - ${err}`));
      alert('Cannot save: Invalid process structure\n\n' + validation.errors.join('\n'));
      return;
    }
    
    addTerminalLog('[OK] Process structure is valid');
    
    try {
      if (!currentProcessId) {
        const result = await saveProcess('Untitled Process', '', nodes, edges, '', '');
        setCurrentProcessId(result.id);
      } else {
        await saveProcess(
          processList.find(p => p.id === currentProcessId)?.name || 'Untitled',
          '',
          nodes,
          edges,
          '',
          ''
        );
      }
      await loadProcessList();
      addTerminalLog('[OK] Process saved successfully');
      alert('Process saved!');
    } catch (error) {
      addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Failed to save: ${error}`);
      alert('Failed to save');
    }
  };

  const loadProcessList = async () => {
    try {
      const result = await listProcesses();
      setProcessList(result.processes || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load processes:', error);
    }
  };

  const loadProcess = async (processId: string) => {
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Loading process: ${processId}`);
    try {
      const process = await getProcess(processId);
      const nodesWithType = process.nodes.map((n: any) => ({ 
        ...n, 
        type: 'custom',
        data: { ...n.data, status: 'pending' } 
      }));
      
      // Валидация загруженного процесса
      const validation = validator.validate(nodesWithType, process.edges);
      if (!validation.valid) {
        addTerminalLog('[WARN] Loaded process has issues:');
        validation.errors.forEach(err => addTerminalLog(`  - ${err}`));
      } else {
        addTerminalLog('[OK] Loaded process structure is valid');
      }
      
      setNodes(nodesWithType);
      setEdges(process.edges);
      setCurrentProcessId(process.id);
      addTerminalLog(`[OK] Loaded process: ${process.name}`);
      
      // Apply current edge routing style to loaded edges
      setTimeout(() => updateEdgesStyle(edgeRouting), 100);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load process:', error);
      addTerminalLog(`[FAIL] Failed to load: ${error}`);
    }
  };

  const handleExecute = async () => {
    if (!currentProcessId) {
      addTerminalLog('[FAIL] Please save the process first');
      alert('Save the process first');
      return;
    }
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Executing process: ${currentProcessId}`);
    await startExecution();
  };

  const handleUpdateNode = (nodeId: string, newData: any) => {
    setNodes((nds) =>
      nds.map((node) =>
        node.id === nodeId ? { ...node, data: newData } : node
      )
    );
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Updated node: ${newData.label || nodeId}`);
  };

  const handleApprove = async (nodeId: string) => {
    if (!execution) return;
    await approveNode({
      nodeId,
      nodeLabel: nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId)?.data.label || '',
      taskId: execution.executionId || '',
      executionId: execution.executionId || ''
    });
    addTerminalLog(`[OK] Approved node: ${nodeId}`);
  };

  const handleAutoLayout = async () => {
    addTerminalLog(`[INFO] Running ${layoutEngine} layout (${layoutDirection})...`);
    
    if (nodes.length === 0) {
      addTerminalLog('[WARN] No nodes to layout');
      return;
    }
    
    // Валидация перед компоновкой
    const validation = validator.validate(nodes, edges);
    if (!validation.valid) {
      addTerminalLog('[ERROR] Cannot layout: Invalid process structure');
      validation.errors.forEach(err => addTerminalLog(`  - ${err}`));
      alert('Cannot layout: Invalid process structure\n\n' + validation.errors.join('\n'));
      return;
    }
    
    addTerminalLog('[OK] Process structure is valid');
    
    console.log('=== LAYOUT DEBUG ===');
    console.log('Engine:', layoutEngine);
    console.log('Nodes before layout:', nodes.map(n => ({ 
      id: n.id, 
      type: n.data.node_type,
      x: n.position.x, 
      y: n.position.y 
    })));
    console.log('Edges:', edges.map(e => ({ source: e.source, target: e.target })));
    
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
    
    let layoutedNodes: Node[];
    
    if (layoutEngine === 'semantic') {
      const resetNodes = nodes.map(node => ({
        ...node,
        position: { x: 0, y: 0 }
      }));
      layoutedNodes = await semanticLayout(resetNodes, edges, {
        horizontalSpacing: 320,
        verticalSpacing: 180,
        branchSpacing: 250
      });
    } else if (layoutEngine === 'elk') {
      layoutedNodes = await smartLayout(nodes, edges, {
        direction: layoutDirection,
        quality: layoutQuality,
        edgeRouting: edgeRouting,
        nodeNodeSpacing: 100,
        layerSpacing: 150,
      });
    } else if (layoutEngine === 'bpmn') {
      layoutedNodes = await bpmnLayout(nodes, edges, {
        horizontalSpacing: 250,
        verticalSpacing: 100,
        branchSpacing: 80,
        parallelSpacing: 60,
      });
    } else {
      // simple layout
      layoutedNodes = await simpleLayoutFn(nodes, edges, {
        stepX: 250,
        stepY: 120,
      });
    }
        
    console.log('Nodes after layout:', layoutedNodes.map(n => ({ 
      id: n.id, 
      type: n.data.node_type,
      x: n.position.x, 
      y: n.position.y 
    })));
    
    const finalNodes = resolveCollisions(layoutedNodes, edges, 20);
    
    setNodes(finalNodes);
    setTimeout(() => updateEdgesStyle(edgeRouting), 100);
    
    addTerminalLog('[OK] Layout completed with collision resolution');
  };

  const handleClearTerminal = () => {
    setTerminalLogs(['> Terminal cleared']);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadProjects();
    loadProcessList();
  }, []);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedProcessId) {
      loadProcess(selectedProcessId);
    }
  }, [selectedProcessId]);
  
  // Force update node dimensions before layout when quality changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (nodes.length > 0 && layoutQuality !== 'fast') {
      setNodes(prev => [...prev]);
    }
  }, [layoutQuality]);

  // Initialize resize for all panels
  useEffect(() => {
    const leftPanel = document.querySelector('.node-palette-minimal');
    const rightPanel = document.querySelector('.right-panel');
    const terminalPanel = document.querySelector('.terminal-panel');
    
    let startX: number, startWidth: number;
    let startY: number, startHeight: number;
    let activePanel: HTMLElement | null = null;
    
    const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!activePanel) return;
      if (activePanel === leftPanel) {
        const newWidth = startWidth + (e.clientX - startX);
        if (newWidth >= 180 && newWidth <= 400) {
          activePanel.style.width = newWidth + 'px';
        }
      } else if (activePanel === rightPanel) {
        const newWidth = startWidth + (startX - e.clientX);
        if (newWidth >= 200 && newWidth <= 500) {
          activePanel.style.width = newWidth + 'px';
        }
      } else if (activePanel === terminalPanel) {
        const newHeight = startHeight + (startY - e.clientY);
        if (newHeight >= 120 && newHeight <= 600) {
          activePanel.style.height = newHeight + 'px';
        }
      }
    };
    
    const onMouseUp = () => {
      activePanel = null;
      document.body.style.cursor = '';
      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
    };
    
    const createResizeHandle = (panel: HTMLElement, direction: string, cursor: string) => {
      const handle = document.createElement('div');
      handle.className = `resize-handle resize-handle-${direction}`;
      handle.style.cssText = `position: absolute; 
        ${direction === 'right' ? 'right: -4px; top: 0; bottom: 0; width: 6px;' : 
          direction === 'left' ? 'left: -4px; top: 0; bottom: 0; width: 6px;' :
          direction === 'top' ? 'top: -4px; left: 0; right: 0; height: 6px;' : 
          'bottom: -4px; left: 0; right: 0; height: 6px;'} 
        cursor: ${cursor}; z-index: 100;`;
      handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        activePanel = panel;
        startX = e.clientX;
        startY = e.clientY;
        startWidth = panel.offsetWidth;
        startHeight = panel.offsetHeight;
        document.body.style.cursor = cursor;
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
        document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
      });
      panel.style.position = 'relative';
      panel.appendChild(handle);
    };
    
    if (leftPanel && !leftPanel.querySelector('.resize-handle')) createResizeHandle(leftPanel as HTMLElement, 'right', 'ew-resize');
    if (rightPanel && !rightPanel.querySelector('.resize-handle')) createResizeHandle(rightPanel as HTMLElement, 'left', 'ew-resize');
    if (terminalPanel && !terminalPanel.querySelector('.resize-handle')) createResizeHandle(terminalPanel as HTMLElement, 'top', 'ns-resize');
    
    return () => {
      const handles = document.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle');
      handles.forEach(h => h.removeEventListener('mousedown', () => {}));
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="process-designer">
      <div className="process-designer-toolbar">
        <h1 className="process-designer-title">Process Designer</h1>
        <div className="project-selector">
          <select className="project-select" value={selectedProject} onChange={(e) => setSelectedProject(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">Select project...</option>
            {projects.map((project) => (
              <option key={project.path} value={project.path}>{project.name}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <button className="project-select-btn" onClick={handleSelectProject}>Open</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="process-designer-main">
        <MinimalNodePalette />
        
        <div className="canvas-terminal-container">
          <CanvasToolbar
            onLayout={handleAutoLayout}
            layoutDirection={layoutDirection}
            onLayoutDirectionChange={setLayoutDirection}
            layoutQuality={layoutQuality}
            onLayoutQualityChange={setLayoutQuality}
            edgeRouting={edgeRouting}
            onEdgeRoutingChange={setEdgeRouting}
            layoutEngine={layoutEngine}
            onLayoutEngineChange={setLayoutEngine}
            onClear={handleClearTerminal}
            onStop={() => addTerminalLog('Stop pressed')}
            onSave={handleSave}
            onRun={handleExecute}
            nodes={nodes}
            edges={edges}
          />
          
          <div className="process-designer-canvas" onDragOver={onDragOver} onDrop={onDrop}>
            <ReactFlow
              nodes={nodes}
              edges={edges}
              onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
              onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
              onConnect={onConnect}
              onNodeClick={(_, node) => setSelectedNode(node)}
              nodeTypes={nodeTypes}            
              defaultEdgeOptions={{
                animated: true,
                style: { stroke: '#5A6E8A', strokeWidth: 2 }
              }}
              nodeDragThreshold={0}
              nodesDraggable={true}
              nodesConnectable={true}
              snapToGrid={true}
              snapGrid={[15, 15]}
              selectionOnDrag={true}
              panOnDrag={[1, 2]}
              deleteKeyCode={null}
              multiSelectionKeyCode="Shift"
              selectionMode="partial"
              onSelectionChange={onSelectionChange}
            >
              <Background color="#1A2538" gap={20} size={1} />
              <Controls className="process-designer-controls" />
              <MiniMap className="process-designer-minimap" nodeColor="#4A9FFF" maskColor="rgba(10, 15, 26, 0.8)" />
            </ReactFlow>
          </div>

          <div className="terminal-panel">
            <div className="terminal-toolbar">
              <div className="terminal-actions-right">
                <button className="terminal-btn" onClick={handleExport}>Export JSON</button>
                <label className="terminal-btn" style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                  Import JSON
                  <input type="file" accept=".json" onChange={handleImport} style={{ display: 'none' }} />
                </label>
                <button className="terminal-btn" onClick={() => addTerminalLog('[INFO] Share feature coming soon')}>Share</button>
                <select className="terminal-process-select" value={selectedProcessId} onChange={(e) => setSelectedProcessId(e.target.value)}>
                  <option value="">Select process...</option>
                  {processList.map((p: any) => (
                    <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="terminal-content">
              {terminalLogs.map((log, idx) => (
                <div key={idx} className="terminal-log-line">{log}</div>
              ))}
              <div ref={terminalEndRef} />
            </div>
            
            <div className="terminal-prompt-container">
              <div
                className="terminal-prompt-input"
                contentEditable
                suppressContentEditableWarning
                data-placeholder="Describe your process in natural language..."
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    const text = e.currentTarget.innerText.trim();
                    if (text) {
                      addTerminalLog(`> ${text}`);
                      setPromptInput(text);
                      e.currentTarget.innerText = '';
                    }
                  }
                }}
                onInput={(e) => setPromptInput(e.currentTarget.innerText)}
              />
              
              <div className="terminal-prompt-actions">
                <button className="terminal-attach-btn" title="Attach">⸬</button>
                <button className="terminal-send-btn" onClick={handleSendPrompt} title="Send">
                  <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" strokeWidth="2">
                    <path d="M12 5v14M5 12l7-7 7 7"/>
                  </svg>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>  
        </div>

        <div className="right-panel">
          <div className="right-panel-tabs">
            <button className="right-panel-tab active" data-tab="agents">Agents</button>
            <button className="right-panel-tab" data-tab="info">Info</button>
            <button className="right-panel-tab" data-tab="settings">Settings</button>
          </div>
          <div className="right-panel-content">
            <div className="agents-tree" id="agentsTab">
              <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">▼</span><span className="tree-label">core_agents</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
              <div className="tree-children">
                <div className="tree-item selected"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">architect_agent</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
                <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">coder_agent</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
                <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">validator_agent</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
              </div>
              <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">▶</span><span className="tree-label">project_agents</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
              <div className="tree-children">
                <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">frontend_developer</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
                <div className="tree-item"><span className="tree-icon">◈</span><span className="tree-label">api_designer</span><div className="tree-actions"><button className="tree-action-btn">...</button></div></div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div id="infoTab" style={{ display: 'none' }}>
              {selectedNode ? (
                <>
                  <div className="info-field"><div className="info-label">Name</div><div className="info-value">{selectedNode.data.label || 'Unnamed'}</div></div>
                  <div className="info-field"><div className="info-label">Type</div><div className="info-value">{selectedNode.data.node_type}</div></div>
                  <div className="info-field"><div className="info-label">Status</div><div className="info-value">{selectedNode.data.status || 'pending'}</div></div>
                  <div className="info-field"><div className="info-label">ID</div><div className="info-value">{selectedNode.id}</div></div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="info-value" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>Select a node to see info</div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div id="settingsTab" style={{ display: 'none' }}>
              <div className="setting-item"><label className="setting-label">Default Model</label><input type="text" className="setting-input" defaultValue="qwen2.5-coder:7b" /></div>
              <div className="setting-item"><label className="setting-label">Temperature</label><input type="range" min="0" max="2" step="0.1" defaultValue="0.7" style={{ width: '100%' }} /></div>
              <div className="setting-item"><label className="setting-label">Max Tokens</label><input type="number" className="setting-input" defaultValue="2000" /></div>
              <div className="setting-item"><label className="setting-label">Ollama URL</label><input type="text" className="setting-input" defaultValue="http://localhost:11434" /></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {execution && execution.status === 'running' && (
          <div className="process-designer-execution-panel">
            <h3>Execution Running</h3>
            <p>Current node: {execution.currentNodeId}</p>
            <div className="execution-progress">
              {Object.entries(execution.nodeStatuses).map(([nodeId, status]) => (
                <div key={nodeId} className={`execution-node-status status-${status}`}>{nodeId}: {status}</div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {selectedNode && selectedNode.data.node_type === 'approval' && (
          <div className="process-designer-inspector">
            <h3 className="process-designer-inspector-title">Approval Required</h3>
            <button onClick={() => handleApprove(selectedNode.id)} className="process-designer-approve-btn">Approve Node</button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ProcessDesignerPage() {
  return (
    <ReactFlowProvider>
      <ProcessDesignerContent />
    </ReactFlowProvider>
  );
}
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Отлично! Валидатор работает и показывает реальные проблемы процесса.

## Проблемы текущего процесса:

1. **Нет START узла** — процесс должен начинаться с START
2. **Нет END узла** — процесс должен заканчиваться END  
3. **agent → agent** — недопустимая связь (нужен промежуточный узел)
4. **JOIN имеет 1 входящий** — должен иметь минимум 2
5. **CONDITION имеет 1 исходящий** — должен иметь минимум 2 (true/false)
6. **PARALLEL не сходится в JOIN** — параллельные ветки должны сливаться

## Как исправить процесс:

```yaml
# Правильная структура должна быть такой:

START → agent_1 (Сборка) → condition_1
                              ├──→ (true)  agent_2 (Тестирование) → END
                              └──→ (false) agent_3 (Деплой) → END

# Или с parallel/join:

START → parallel_1
           ├──→ agent_1 → ┐
           ├──→ agent_2 → ├→ join_1 → END
           └──→ agent_3 → ┘
```

- Добавить START перед узлом `3`
- Добавить END после узлов `1` и `4`
- Убрать прямые связи agent→agent
- Добавить второй выход для CONDITION
- Добавить второй вход для JOIN

```
START → agent (Сборка) → condition
                           ├→ agent (Тестирование) → END
                           └→ agent (Деплой) → END
```

1. Перетащи `Start` на канвас

## Шаг 1: Проверка ИИ-генератора

Выполни в терминале:

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3
curl -X POST http://localhost:3001/api/processes/generate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"requirement": "Create a simple process: start, then agent for code review, then condition: if approved go to deploy agent and end, if rejected go to fix agent and end"}'
```

**Или через UI:** Введи в терминале (в правой панели) тот же промпт.

## Шаг 2: Проверка на соответствие правилам

После генерации, в консоли браузера должны быть логи:

```
[OK] Generated process structure is valid
```

Или ошибки с пояснениями.

## Шаг 3: Тестовые промпты для ИИ

Проверь эти сценарии:

### ✅ Должен работать:
```
Create a process with start, then bash script, then condition with two branches: success branch goes to http request then end, failure branch goes to agent then end
```

### ❌ Должен выдавать ошибку:
```
Create a process with start, then agent, then another agent, then end
```
(Ошибка: agent cannot connect to agent)

### ❌ Должен выдавать ошибку:
```
Create a process with start, then condition with only one output to agent, then end
```
(Ошибка: CONDITION must have at least 2 outgoing connections)

## Шаг 4: Проверка API генератора

Покажи содержимое файла:
```bash
cat /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/src/features/process-designer/api/processApi.ts
```

Нужно убедиться, что `generateProcess` на бэкенде использует правила валидации.

## Шаг 5: Если ИИ генерирует невалидные процессы

Нужно улучшить промпт для ИИ. Добавь в `generateProcess` на бэкенде системный промпт:

```python
# На бэкенде (пример для Ollama)
system_prompt = """
You are a BPMN process designer. Generate valid process structures following these rules:

1. Every process must have exactly one START node and at least one END node
2. Valid node types: start, end, agent, task, condition, parallel, join, subprocess, http_node, bash, git, approval, try_catch, for_each, while_loop, set_variable
3. Connections rules:
   - START can connect to: agent, task, condition, parallel, subprocess, http_node, bash, git, approval
   - agent can connect to: agent, task, condition, parallel, join, end, subprocess, http_node, bash, git, approval
   - condition MUST have 2+ outgoing connections
   - join MUST have 2+ incoming connections
   - parallel MUST converge to a join node
   - END cannot have outgoing connections
4. Return JSON with { nodes: [...], edges: [...] }
5. Each node must have: id, type, position (x, y), data { label, node_type, status: "pending" }
6. Each edge must have: id, source, target
"""
```

**Покажи результат тестов.** Если ИИ генерирует невалидные процессы — будем исправлять промпты. Если валидатор работает — отлично, этап 70 почти завершён.

const API_BASE = 'http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process';

export interface ProcessNode {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  position: { x: number; y: number };
  data: Record<string, any>;
}

export interface ProcessEdge {
  id: string;
  source: string;
  target: string;
  animated?: boolean;
  style?: any;
}

export interface Process {
  id?: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  nodes: ProcessNode[];
  edges: ProcessEdge[];
  yaml?: string;
  mermaid?: string;
}

// Получить все типы узлов (плагины)
export async function getNodeTypes() {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/node-types`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch node types');
  return response.json();
}

// Генерация процесса через AI
export async function generateProcess(requirement: string) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/design`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ requirement })
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to generate process');
  return response.json();
}

// Сохранение процесса
export async function saveProcess(name: string, description: string, nodes: ProcessNode[], edges: ProcessEdge[], yaml: string = '', mermaid: string = '') {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/save`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ name, description, nodes, edges, yaml, mermaid })
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to save process');
  return response.json();
}

// Получить список процессов
export async function listProcesses() {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/list`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to list processes');
  return response.json();
}

// Получить процесс по ID
export async function getProcess(processId: string) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/${processId}`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to get process');
  return response.json();
}

// Запустить выполнение процесса
export async function executeProcess(processId: string) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/execute/${processId}`, {
    method: 'POST'
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to execute process');
  return response.json();
}

// Получить статус выполнения
export async function getExecutionStatus(executionId: string) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/execution/${executionId}/status`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to get execution status');
  return response.json();
}

## 📋 НЕОБХОДИМЫЕ ДЕЙСТВИЯ:

### Вариант А: Создать минимальный бэкенд (рекомендую)

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3
mkdir -p server
cd server
npm init -y
npm install express cors dotenv
```

**Создать `server/index.js`:**

```javascript
const express = require('express');
const cors = require('cors');
const app = express();
const port = 8010;

app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json());

// Моковые данные
let processes = [];
let nodeTypes = {
  node_types: {
    agent: { type: 'agent', ui_config: { label: 'Agent', category: 'ai', bg_color: '#8B5CF6' } },
    condition: { type: 'condition', ui_config: { label: 'Condition', category: 'control', bg_color: '#10B981' } },
    parallel: { type: 'parallel', ui_config: { label: 'Parallel', category: 'control', bg_color: '#2DD4BF' } },
    join: { type: 'join', ui_config: { label: 'Join', category: 'control', bg_color: '#4A9FFF' } },
    start: { type: 'start', ui_config: { label: 'Start', category: 'control', bg_color: '#2DD4BF' } },
    end: { type: 'end', ui_config: { label: 'End', category: 'control', bg_color: '#EF4444' } },
    approval: { type: 'approval', ui_config: { label: 'Approval', category: 'integration', bg_color: '#F59E0B' } },
  }
};

// GET /api/v1/process/node-types
app.get('/api/v1/process/node-types', (req, res) => {
  res.json(nodeTypes);
});

// POST /api/v1/process/design
app.post('/api/v1/process/design', (req, res) => {
  const { requirement } = req.body;
  
  // Простая генерация процесса
  const nodes = [
    { id: 'start', type: 'custom', position: { x: 100, y: 100 }, data: { label: 'Start', node_type: 'start', status: 'pending' } },
    { id: 'task1', type: 'custom', position: { x: 350, y: 100 }, data: { label: requirement.slice(0, 30), node_type: 'agent', status: 'pending' } },
    { id: 'end', type: 'custom', position: { x: 600, y: 100 }, data: { label: 'End', node_type: 'end', status: 'pending' } }
  ];
  
  const edges = [
    { id: 'e1', source: 'start', target: 'task1' },
    { id: 'e2', source: 'task1', target: 'end' }
  ];
  
  res.json({ nodes, edges, yaml: '', mermaid: '' });
});

// POST /api/v1/process/save
app.post('/api/v1/process/save', (req, res) => {
  const { name, description, nodes, edges } = req.body;
  const id = Date.now().toString();
  const process = { id, name, description, nodes, edges, createdAt: new Date() };
  processes.push(process);
  res.json({ id, message: 'Process saved' });
});

// GET /api/v1/process/list
app.get('/api/v1/process/list', (req, res) => {
  res.json({ processes });
});

// GET /api/v1/process/:id
app.get('/api/v1/process/:id', (req, res) => {
  const process = processes.find(p => p.id === req.params.id);
  if (process) res.json(process);
  else res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found' });
});

// POST /api/v1/process/execute/:id
app.post('/api/v1/process/execute/:id', (req, res) => {
  res.json({ executionId: Date.now().toString(), status: 'started' });
});

app.listen(port, () => {
  console.log(`✅ BPMN Server running on http://localhost:${port}`);
});
```

**Запустить:**
```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command-v3/server
node index.js
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

Отлично! **Бэкенд существует и работает!**

## Результаты аудита:

### ✅ **РАБОТАЕТ:**
1. **Бэкенд на Python** запущен на порту 8010 (два процесса: PID 16397 и 21756)
2. **Оркестратор Batuta** есть в `/ai-farm/agents/batuta/`
3. **API эндпоинты** должны быть доступны

### 🔍 **Что нужно проверить:**

Выполни эти команды для проверки API:

```bash
# 1. Проверить, что бэкенд отвечает
curl http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/node-types

# 2. Проверить генерацию процесса
curl -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/design \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"requirement": "simple process with start agent and end"}'

# 3. Проверить список процессов
curl http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/list

# 4. Посмотреть логи бэкенда (если есть)
tail -50 /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/logs/*.log 2>/dev/null || echo "No logs"

# 5. Проверить основной файл API
cat /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/ai-farm/agents/batuta/core/api.py | head -100
```

**Покажи вывод.** Если API работает, значит:
- Фронтенд правильно настроен на localhost:8010
- Оркестратор готов принимать запросы
- Нужно только убедиться, что генерация процессов учитывает наши правила валидации

Генерация процесса ===\" RESULT=$(curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/design \\   -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\   -d '{\"requirement\": \"Создай процесс: анализ, код, проверка\"}')  echo \"=== 2. Сохранение процесса ===\" NODES=$(echo $RESULT | python3 -c \"import sys, json; print(json.dumps(json.load(sys.stdin)['nodes']))\") EDGES=$(echo $RESULT | python3 -c \"import sys, json; print(json.dumps(json.load(sys.stdin)['edges']))\") YAML=$(echo $RESULT | python3 -c \"import sys, json; print(json.dumps(json.load(sys.stdin)['yaml']))\") MERMAID=$(echo $RESULT | python3 -c \"import sys, json; print(json.dumps(json.load(sys.stdin)['mermaid']))\")  SAVE_RESULT=$(curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/save \\   -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\   -d \"{\\\"name\\\": \\\"Test Process\\\", \\\"description\\\": \\\"Test\\\", \\\"nodes\\\": $NODES, \\\"edges\\\": $EDGES, \\\"yaml\\\": $YAML, \\\"mermaid\\\": $MERMAID}\") PROCESS_ID=$(echo $SAVE_RESULT | python3 -c \"import sys, json; print(json.load(sys.stdin).get('id', ''))\") echo \"Process ID: $PROCESS_ID\"  echo \"=== 3. Запуск выполнения ===\" EXEC_RESULT=$(curl -s -X POST http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/execute/$PROCESS_ID) EXECUTION_ID=$(echo $EXEC_RESULT | python3 -c \"import sys, json; print(json.load(sys.stdin).get('execution_id', ''))\") echo \"Execution ID: $EXECUTION_ID\"  echo \"=== 4. Проверка статуса (ждём 5 секунд) ===\" sleep 5 curl -s http://localhost:8010/api/v1/process/execution/$EXECUTION_ID/status | python3 -m json.tool","created_at":"2026-04-20 15:39:09","updated_at":"2026-04-20 15:39:09"},{"id":"3b663361","name":"AI Generated: Процесс clipping комманд из deepseek чата и их вып","description":"Процесс clipping комманд из deepseek чата и их выполнение в кодовой базе проекта","created_at":"2026-04-20 15:35:14","updated_at":"2026-04-20 15:35:14"},{"id":"aab24d90","name":"AI Generated: Создай процесс: анализ кода, проверка с аппровом, ","description":"Создай процесс: анализ кода, проверка с аппровом, генерация фикса, интеграция","created_at":"2026-04-20 13:39:01","updated_at":"2026-04-20 13:39:01"},{"id":"49652d9c","name":"Test Process","description":"Generated test process","created_at":"2026-04-20 13:30:03","updated_at":"2026-04-20 13:30:03"}]}zsh: unknown file attribute: ^
